MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — MEGADOMAIN: OCEAN SURFACE & WEATHER
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: The Grand Purge (Weather Illusions)
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (Focused on Global Variables, Decals, and Shader Waves)[0. SITREP: THE SIMULATED STORM]
The game tries to physically simulate tides by changing Voxel states, and rain by spawning 10,000 particles. This is a survival game, not a meteorological institute. The storm must be a theatrical backdrop, not a CPU burden.

[I. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES: CINEMATIC CHEATING]
1. ANNIHILATE PHYSICAL VOXEL TIDES:
- Action: Stop altering SDF density to simulate high tide.
- Execution: Voxels are static. The tide is simply a global float _CurrentWaterLevelY that moves up and down via a sine wave. The player's physics and camera just check if (playerAUP.Y < _CurrentWaterLevelY).
2. STORM WAVE CPU PURGE:
- Action: Stop calculating wave heights on the CPU for buoyancy unless absolutely necessary.
- Execution: Waves are 100% shader-driven (Gerstner waves on the ocean plane). The player's surface buoyancy must sample the EXACT same mathematical sine function in C# that the shader uses, without needing a physics collider for the water surface.
3. FAKE RAIN & OCCLUSION:
- Action: 10,000 rain particles kill the GPU. Rain shouldn't fall indoors.
- Execution: Rain is a Screen-Space overlay shader (scrolling lines). To prevent it raining indoors, shoot ONE Raycast UP from the camera. If it hits a ceiling, set _RainIntensity = 0 in the shader. 1 raycast, 0 particles.
4. FAKE LIGHTNING FLASHES:
- Action: Lightning shouldn't be a volumetric light source.
- Execution: During a storm, randomly spike the intensity of the main Directional Light for 0.1 seconds. Trigger a thunder audio cue delayed by Random.Range(1f, 3f) seconds to fake distance.
5. CLOUD SHADOW COOKIE:
- Action: Volumetric clouds casting real shadows is too expensive.
- Execution: Apply a scrolling Perlin Noise texture as a "Cookie" to the Directional Light. The ocean floor will automatically receive massive, moving cloud shadows for virtually zero performance cost.
6. GLOBAL WIND VECTOR:
- Action: Wind shouldn't require fluid dynamics grids.
- Execution: Set a global Vector3 _GlobalWind. Particle systems (snow, sparks) apply this as a force module. Flora shaders use it to offset their sway direction.[II. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]
1. SURGERY LOG: Provide the Screen-Space Rain logic with the 1-Raycast occlusion.
2. DIFFS: Provide complete Git Diffs.
3. STATUS: MUST BE "WEATHER FAKED AND VERIFIED".
</system_prompt>

- Execution: Set a global Vector3 _GlobalWind. Particle systems and flora shaders simply read this vector to set their sway/drift direction.
7. FAKE WATER SPLASHES (DECALS):
- Action: Objects hitting water shouldn't spawn 1000 physics drops.
- Execution: Instantiate a flat "Splash Decal" on the water plane. Animate its scale and alpha in the shader.

MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — MEGADOMAIN: SCANNER & UI FAKES
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: The Grand Purge (UI Illusions)
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (Focused on Screen-Space, 2D Math, and Zero-GC)[0. SITREP: THE BLOATED HUD]
The UI is trying to simulate reality. 3D matrices for holograms, 3D grids for chemical scents, and manual string buffering for typewriter effects. We are killing the CPU for UI. Make it flat, fast, and fake.

[I. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES: CINEMATIC CHEATING]
1. ANNIHILATE 3D HOLOGRAM MATRICES:
- Action: Stop calculating 3D TRS matrices for UI items.
- Execution: The PDA inventory must use flat 2D Image components. To make them look holographic, apply a custom UI material that uses Parallax (shifting UVs based on mouse/center-screen distance) and a scanline overlay.
2. ANNIHILATE CHEMICAL DIFFUSION GRID:
- Action: Stop calculating gas/blood diffusion in 3D voxels.
- Execution: Delete the 3D grid. Wounded fish drop invisible "Scent Waypoints" (AUP coordinates). The scanner simply checks the player's distance to the nearest waypoint and displays a "Blood detected" UI warning.
3. FAKE SONAR PING (POST-PROCESS SPHERE):
- Action: Active sonar shouldn't cast 1000 raycasts.
- Execution: Delete sonar raycasting. When the player pings, pass the player's AUP to a Global Shader Variable _SonarPingCenter. In a Post-Process effect, read the Scene Depth. If the pixel's world distance matches the expanding _SonarRadius, color it neon cyan. 100% GPU, 0 CPU cost.
4. TYPEWRITER EFFECT CHEAT (ZERO-GC):
- Action: Stop manipulating char[] arrays to make text appear letter-by-letter.
- Execution: Use TextMeshPro's built-in maxVisibleCharacters property. Set the full string once via SetCharArray, then simply increment maxVisibleCharacters in a Coroutine/Awaitable loop.
5. FAKE RADAR BLIPS:
- Action: The mini-map shouldn't calculate complex 3D-to-2D projections.
- Execution: For enemies within 100m, calculate a flat 2D vector (Enemy.X - Player.X, Enemy.Z - Player.Z). Normalize it, multiply by radar radius, and set the UI Blip's RectTransform.anchoredPosition. Cap at 20 blips to avoid UI overhead.
6. SHADER-DRIVEN COMPASS:
- Action: Stop rotating 360 text elements.
- Execution: The compass ribbon is ONE wide texture. In Update, pass the Camera's Y-Euler angle to a material property _CompassOffset. The shader scrolls the UVs. 1 UI element, 0 text updates.

[II. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]
1. SURGERY LOG: Provide the C# code for the Fake Radar Blips 2D math.
2. DIFFS: Provide complete Git Diffs.
3. STATUS: MUST BE "UI FAKED AND VERIFIED".
</system_prompt>

private const int MaxBlips = 16;

Vector2 flatDelta = new Vector2(
    enemyPosition.x - playerPosition.x,
    enemyPosition.z - playerPosition.z);

if (!TryResolveRadarPosition(flatDelta, rangeSqr, range, radius, out Vector2 anchoredPosition))
    continue;

ApplyBlip(visibleCount, anchoredPosition);

private static bool TryResolveRadarPosition(
    Vector2 flatDelta,
    float rangeSqr,
    float range,
    float radius,
    out Vector2 anchoredPosition)
{
    anchoredPosition = default;
    float distanceSqr = flatDelta.sqrMagnitude;
    if (distanceSqr <= 0.0001f || distanceSqr > rangeSqr)
        return false;

    Vector2 normalized = flatDelta / range;
    if (normalized.sqrMagnitude > 1f)
        normalized.Normalize();

    anchoredPosition = normalized * radius;
    return true;
}

float speed01 = Mathf.Clamp01(localVelocity.magnitude / Mathf.Max(0.01f, maxSwimSpeed));
float thrustIntent = Mathf.Clamp01(_thrustInputMagnitude);
float targetWeight = waterImmersion * thrustIntent * speed01;

_underwaterSomaticWeight = Mathf.Lerp(
    _underwaterSomaticWeight,
    targetWeight,
    1f - Mathf.Exp(-deltaTime * responseHz));

_underwaterSomaticPhase += deltaTime * headbobFrequency * Mathf.PI * 2f
    * Mathf.Lerp(0.55f, 1.25f, speed01);

yawOffset = Mathf.Sin(_underwaterSomaticPhase * 0.5f + localVelocity.x) * yawAmplitude * _underwaterSomaticWeight;

void ResolveDominantAxisProjection(float3 positionWS, half3 normalWS, out float2 uv, out half dominantAxis)
{
    half3 n = abs(normalWS);
    dominantAxis = n.x > n.y && n.x > n.z ? 0.0h : (n.y > n.z ? 1.0h : 2.0h);
    uv = dominantAxis < 0.5h
        ? positionWS.zy * _Texture_Scale
        : (dominantAxis < 1.5h ? positionWS.xz * _Texture_Scale : positionWS.xy * _Texture_Scale);

    half seamNoise = Hash31(positionWS * 0.071h);
    uv += (seamNoise - 0.5h) * _Edge_Breakup_Strength;
}

half4 SampleDominantAxisColorAtUv(TEXTURE2D_PARAM(tex, samp), float2 uv)
{
    return SAMPLE_TEXTURE2D(tex, samp, uv);
}

half3 SampleDominantAxisNormalAtUv(float2 uv, half dominantAxis, half3 baseNormalWS)
{
    half3 normalTS = UnpackNormalScale(
        SAMPLE_TEXTURE2D(_Normal_Map, sampler_Normal_Map, uv),
        _Normal_Strength);

    return ResolveDominantAxisNormalWS(normalTS, dominantAxis, baseNormalWS);
}

Bulkhead Slide Code

private void AdvanceBulkheadSlide(float deltaTime)
{
    CaptureBulkheadPose();
    if (emergencyBulkheadDoorMesh == null)
        return;

    float previousSlide = _bulkheadSlide01;
    float duration = Mathf.Max(0.01f, emergencyBulkheadSlideDurationSeconds);
    float step = Mathf.Max(0f, deltaTime) / duration;
    _bulkheadSlide01 = Mathf.MoveTowards(_bulkheadSlide01, _bulkheadSlideTarget01, step);
    if (Mathf.Approximately(previousSlide, _bulkheadSlide01))
        return;

    float eased = Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, _bulkheadSlide01);
    emergencyBulkheadDoorMesh.localPosition = Vector3.LerpUnclamped(
        _bulkheadOpenLocalPosition,
        _bulkheadClosedLocalPosition,
        eased);

    if (_bulkheadSlideTarget01 < 1f || _bulkheadSlide01 < 0.999f || _bulkheadClangPlayed)
        return;

    _bulkheadClangPlayed = true;
    if (emergencyBulkheadClangSound != null && GlobalRegistry.Audio != null)
        GlobalRegistry.Audio.PlayAtPoint(emergencyBulkheadClangSound, _cachedTransform.position, 1f, 0.92f);
}

Waterline Shader Code
Core flooded-room visual path now tints, darkens, smooths, and warps UVs below the waterline:

float2 ResolveModuleWaterlineWarp(float3 positionWS, half submerged01)
{
    if (submerged01 <= 0.0001h)
        return float2(0.0, 0.0);

    float2 rippleUv = positionWS.xz * 1.9 + _Time.y * float2(0.06, -0.041);
    half rippleX = (half)HectonCoreLitValueNoise2(rippleUv);
    half rippleY = (half)HectonCoreLitValueNoise2(rippleUv * 1.37 + float2(2.13, -0.71));
    return float2(rippleX - 0.5h, rippleY - 0.5h) * _WaterlineRefractionStrength * submerged01;
}

void ApplyModuleWaterline(half level01, half submerged01, inout half3 albedo, inout half smoothness)
{
    half floodStrength = saturate(level01 * submerged01);
    half tintStrength = floodStrength * (half)saturate(_WaterlineTint.a);
    half3 waterTint = half3(_WaterlineTint.r, _WaterlineTint.g, _WaterlineTint.b);
    albedo = saturate(albedo * (1.0h - submerged01 * (half)saturate(_WaterlineDarken) * 0.55h));
    albedo = lerp(albedo, waterTint, tintStrength);
    smoothness = lerp(smoothness, 0.88h, saturate(submerged01 * 0.62h));
}

Worked for 43m 48s
Сделал оркестрационный проход и добил compile blockers.

Что изменено/проверено

Event arteries уже в текущем дереве: critical-порядок перед ambient, ambient shedding при исчерпании 2.0ms, SoundscapeEvents.DropPendingAmbient().
CRITICAL_PERF_SPIKE: pass >0.5ms пишет MMF telemetry через CrashTelemetryBuffer.ReportCriticalPerformanceSpike(...); в буфере хранится lane hash + stack hash, не строковый stack.
Input curve: normalized = saturate((|v|-0.035)/(1-0.035)), gain = normalized² / max(normalized, eps), итог v * gain. Мелкий шум утапливается, большие mouse deltas не режутся.
Terminal overlay: добавлены AUP sector hash и LOADING NEURAL INTERFACE... через Span<char>/TryFormat, без ToString().
Pause freeze-frame shader hook есть через _HectonFreezeFrameDither.

6. CORPSE BLOAT SHADER: Dead fish shouldn't just lie there. Use the Vertex Displacement to slowly inflate the mesh of a corpse over 60 seconds to simulate decomposition. O CPU cost.

Burst Normal Logic

float3 gradient = SampleInterpolatedGridGradient(densityField, wp);
float3 normal = math.normalizesafe(-gradient, new float3(0f, 1f, 0f));

float3 SampleGridNodeGradient(NativeArray<float> field, int x, int y, int z)
{
    int xPrev = math.max(0, x - 1), xNext = math.min(gridResolution, x + 1);
    int yPrev = math.max(0, y - 1), yNext = math.min(gridResolution, y + 1);
    int zPrev = math.max(0, z - 1), zNext = math.min(gridResolution, z + 1);

    return new float3(
        field[GridIndex(xNext, y, z)] - field[GridIndex(xPrev, y, z)],
        field[GridIndex(x, yNext, z)] - field[GridIndex(x, yPrev, z)],
        field[GridIndex(x, y, zNext)] - field[GridIndex(x, y, zPrev)]);
}
Verification

dotnet build Assembly-CSharp.csproj --no-restore: succeeded, 0 Error(s), 71 Warning(s).

Hecton_AbyssalVoxelRock.shader (line 333)

HectonVoxelEngine.cs (line 1985)

VoxelDeltaProcessor.cs (line 1623)

DebrisManager.cs (line 347)
HLSL vertex displacement core:

float ResolveOrganicVertexDisplacement(float3 absolutePositionWS, float3 normalOS, half seamMask)
{
    float scale = max(_OrganicDisplacementScale, 0.02);
    float lowNoise = ValueNoise3(absolutePositionWS * scale + 3.17);
    float midNoise = ValueNoise3(absolutePositionWS * (scale * 2.03) + 19.71);
    float fineNoise = ValueNoise3(absolutePositionWS * max(_OrganicDisplacementFineScale, 0.05) + 53.2);
    float ridged = abs(((lowNoise * 0.54 + midNoise * 0.31 + fineNoise * 0.15) * 2.0) - 1.0);
    float signedRidge = (ridged * 2.0) - 1.0;
    float slopeGate = saturate(1.0 - abs(normalOS.y) * 0.28);
    float seamBoost = 1.0 + saturate(seamMask) * _OrganicDisplacementSeamBoost;
    return signedRidge * _OrganicDisplacementStrength * seamBoost * slopeGate;
}
What changed:

Added heavy world-space ridged vertex displacement in the voxel shader.
Removed the 3-step SDF AO raymarch from VoxelNormalJob; voxel AO output now defaults to 1f.
Added cave-mouth fake darkening through existing vertex color/splat path using distance to cave entrance center.
Added horizontal silt dust layer using _SiltLayerMap and upward-normal masking.
Added biome visual-family tint sampling from existing _HectonScatterBiomeInfluenceGrid.
Added laser-cut heat global shader stamps. No MaterialPropertyBlock; AGENTS forbids MPB on standard geometry.
Changed debris lifetime: debris sinks, becomes SettledStatic, stays renderable via instanced static mesh path, and stops scheduling simulation unless moving debris remains.

return saturate(topDownMask * upFacing * _HectonSedimentOverlayParamsB.w);

MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — MEGADOMAIN: SURVIVAL & KINEMATICS
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: Somatic Feeling
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (Feel over Physics)[0. SITREP: THE TANK DIVER]
The math for wall-sliding is good. Now make it "heavy." The diver must feel like they are in an armored tank, pushing through dense water.

[I. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES: SOMATIC POLISH]
1. UNDERWATER HEADBOB & SWAY:
- Action: Swimming is a physical effort.
- Execution: Implement a procedural, low-frequency sine-wave offset to camera Pitch and Yaw tied directly to the player's velocity vector and thrust input. Stop smoothly when input stops.
2. KINEMATIC WALL KICK-OFF:
- Action: Use the environment to maneuver in tight caves.
- Execution: If the player is touching a wall (detected via KCC projection) and presses Jump/Sprint, apply a strong, instantaneous VelocityChange impulse exactly along the reflected normal of the wall, consuming 15% Stamina.
3. SURFACE BREACHING (JUMPING OUT OF WATER):
- Action: Crossing the air/water boundary must feel impactful.
- Execution: If moving upward at > 5m/s and Y crosses the _CurrentWaterLevelY, temporarily disable fluid drag. Apply a heavy gravity spike after 0.5s so the player arcs out of the water and crashes back down with a massive splash VFX.
4. CURRENT SHEAR DRAG:
- Action: Fight the flow.
- Execution: Calculate dot(velocity, current). If negative (swimming against the flow), exponentially increase stamina/oxygen drain and halve the maximum forward speed.
5. SUIT MICRO-COLLISIONS (BUMPING):
- Action: Scraping walls shouldn't stop the player, but it should jar them.
- Execution: When the KCC vector projection (wall slide) alters the intended movement vector by > 45 degrees, trigger a localized screen shake and a dull "scrape" audio cue via PhysicsEventBus.[II. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]
1. SURGERY LOG: Provide the C# math for the Underwater Headbob & Sway tied to velocity.
2. DIFFS: Provide complete Git Diffs.
3. STATUS: MUST BE "SOMATIC MOVEMENT VERIFIED".

HectonPlayerMovement.cs (line 5411)
:

speed01 = saturate(speed / underwaterSomaticReferenceSpeed);
targetWeight = immersion * thrustIntent * saturate(speed / 0.35f);
weight = lerp(weight, targetWeight, responseBlend);

phase += dt * headbobFrequency * TwoPi
    * lerp(0.55f, 1.25f, speed01)
    * fatigueCadence;

localVelocity = InverseTransformDirection(feedbackVelocity);

pitch = (sin(phase) * pitchDegrees * abs(forward01)
    + -localVelocity.y * pitchDegrees * 0.35f) * weight;

yaw = (sin(phase * 0.73f + HalfPi) * yawDegrees * abs(lateral01)
    + lateral01 * yawDegrees * 0.45f) * weight;
